markdown">\<header\>\<h1\>Store files in S3</h1></header><div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 3.14-7 7-7z"></path></svg></span>Prerequisites</div><div class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p>You need a module and a client connected to it, and an Amazon S3 bucket, or a bucket on an S3-compatible service such as Cloudflare R2. If you don&#x27;t have a module yet, follow the <a class="" href="/docs/">quickstart</a> for your language first.</p></div></div>
<p>In this guide, you&#x27;ll let players upload files, list them, download them and delete them. The files go straight from the client to your bucket, so large files never pass through SpacetimeDB, and your database only keeps a record of each file. Your module signs short-lived URLs that each allow one kind of request on one file, so your S3 secret key never reaches the client. You&#x27;ll sign them in <a class="" href="/docs/functions/procedures">procedures</a>, with the keys stored in <a class="" href="/docs/databases/environment-variables">environment variables</a>.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="how-it-works">How it works<a href="#how-it-works" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to How it works" title="Direct link to How it works" translate="no">​</a></h2>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</code> and <code>S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</code> environment variables store your S3 keys on the database.</li>
<li class="">A <code>presign</code> function signs a URL for one kind of request on one file, such as an upload, valid for 5 minutes.</li>
<li class="">A <code>request_upload</code> procedure checks the file&#x27;s size, chooses where the file goes in the bucket, records it in a private <code>pending_upload</code> table, and returns a signed upload URL for a file of that size.</li>
<li class="">The client uploads the file to that URL with an HTTP <code>PUT</code>.</li>
<li class="">A <code>confirm_upload</code> procedure checks that the file is in the bucket and isn&#x27;t too big, then moves it to the public <code>uploaded_file</code> table.</li>
<li class="">A <code>request_download</code> procedure returns a signed download URL, and a <code>delete_file</code> procedure deletes a file from the bucket and from <code>uploaded_file</code>.</li>
<li class="">Once an hour, a <code>clean_up_uploads</code> procedure deletes the uploads that players requested but never confirmed.</li>
</ul>
<p>The bucket stays private: anyone who wants a file needs a URL that your module signed. The upload URL also fixes the file&#x27;s size and content type, so the bucket rejects a file bigger than the one the player asked to upload.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="set-up-the-bucket">Set up the bucket<a href="#set-up-the-bucket" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Set up the bucket" title="Direct link to Set up the bucket" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>In your storage service:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">Create a bucket, and keep it private.</li>
<li class="">Create an access key that can only list, read, write and delete files in that bucket. On AWS, that&#x27;s an IAM user with the <code>s3:GetObject</code>, <code>s3:PutObject</code> and <code>s3:DeleteObject</code> permissions on <code>arn:aws:s3:::your-bucket/*</code>, and the <code>s3:ListBucket</code> permission on <code>arn:aws:s3:::your-bucket</code>. Without <code>s3:ListBucket</code>, S3 answers a request for a file that doesn&#x27;t exist with an access error, so <code>confirm_upload</code> can&#x27;t tell that the file was never uploaded.</li>
<li class="">If your client runs in a web browser, add a CORS rule to the bucket that allows <code>PUT</code> and <code>GET</code> requests, and the <code>Content-Type</code> header, from your site&#x27;s origin. Browsers block the upload otherwise.</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="define-the-tables-and-the-keys">Define the tables and the keys<a href="#define-the-tables-and-the-keys" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Define the tables and the keys" title="Direct link to Define the tables and the keys" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Declare the <code>S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</code> and <code>S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</code> environment variables. They&#x27;re required, so SpacetimeDB refuses to publish the module without them.</p>
<p>Then add three tables:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>pending_upload</code> is private. It has one row per upload that a player requested but hasn&#x27;t confirmed yet, with the file&#x27;s name and content type. Its primary key is the file&#x27;s key: its path in the bucket.</li>
<li class=""><code>uploaded_file</code> is public, so clients can subscribe to it and list the files. It has one row per uploaded file, with its owner, key, name, content type, size and upload time.</li>
<li class=""><code>upload_cleanup</code> is the schedule table that runs <code>clean_up_uploads</code>, as described in <a href="#clean-up-abandoned-uploads" class="">Clean up abandoned uploads</a>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { ScheduleAt, TimeDuration } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { schema, table, t, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> InferSchema, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ProcedureCtx } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">spacetimedb/server</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { hmac } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">@noble/hashes/hmac.js</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { sha256 } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">@noble/hashes/sha2.js</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { bytesToHex, utf8ToBytes } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">@noble/hashes/utils.js</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pendingUpload </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">pending_upload</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    requestedAt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">uploaded_file</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">primaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">autoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    scheduledAt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">scheduleAt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> schema</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">type</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ProcedureCtx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">InferSchema</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">typeof</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb>>;</span></span></code></pre><p>Add <code>@noble/hashes</code> to your module&#x27;s dependencies, to compute the signatures:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">npm</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> install</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> @noble/hashes</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">#pragma</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> warning</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> disable</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> STDB_UNSTABLE</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> System</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Globalization</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> System</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Text</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Org</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BouncyCastle</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Crypto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Digests</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Org</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BouncyCastle</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Crypto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Macs</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Org</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">BouncyCastle</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Crypto</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Parameters</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Env</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ModuleEnv</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID;</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PendingUpload</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PendingUpload</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Key;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ContentType;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> RequestedAt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">UploadedFile</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Public </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> true</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadedFile</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Id;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Owner;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Key;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Name;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ContentType;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Size;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> UploadedAt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">UploadCleanup</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, Scheduled </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">CleanUpUploads</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, ScheduledAt </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">ScheduledAt</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadCleanup</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">PrimaryKey</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">AutoInc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ScheduledId;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ScheduleAt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ScheduledAt;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Add the <code>BouncyCastle.Cryptography</code> package to your module, to compute the signatures. The .NET classes in <code>System.Security.Cryptography</code> don&#x27;t work in modules, because modules run on WebAssembly:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">dotnet</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> add</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> package</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> BouncyCastle.Cryptography</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> hmac</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Hmac</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Mac</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> sha2</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Digest</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">http</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Timeout</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{procedure, reducer, table, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ScheduleAt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">time</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Duration</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[spacetimedb</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">env]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Env</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    pub</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    pub</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending_upload)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> PendingUpload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    requested_at</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> uploaded_file, public)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadedFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[auto_inc]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    uploaded_at</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Timestamp</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[table(accessor </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> upload_cleanup, scheduled(clean_up_uploads))]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadCleanup</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[primary_key]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    #[auto_inc]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    scheduled_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    scheduled_at</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ScheduleAt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Add <code>hmac</code> and <code>sha2</code> to your module&#x27;s <code>Cargo.toml</code>, to compute the signatures:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">dependencies</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD">hmac</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">0.12</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD">sha2</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">0.10</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>Set the keys when you publish, by putting them in the publishing command&#x27;s environment:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#BD93F9">S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">your-access-key-id</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">your-secret-access-key</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> spacetime</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> publish</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> my-game</span></span></code></pre>
<p>The keys stay in the database across later publishes, so you only pass them once. See <a class="" href="/docs/databases/environment-variables#supply-values-when-publishing">Environment Variables</a> for other ways to supply them, and to replace them.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="sign-requests">Sign requests<a href="#sign-requests" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Sign requests" title="Direct link to Sign requests" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add a <code>presign</code> function that signs a URL for one kind of request, such as a <code>PUT</code> or a <code>GET</code>, on one file. Anyone who has the URL can make that request until it expires, 5 minutes later, without your keys. It uses <a href="https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/API/sigv4-query-string-auth.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" class="">AWS Signature Version 4</a>, which S3-compatible services also accept:</p>
\<ol\>
<li class="">It builds the request&#x27;s canonical form: the method, the file&#x27;s path, the signature&#x27;s parameters and the signed headers, one per line.</li>
<li class="">It derives a signing key from your secret key, the date, the region and the service, with HMAC-SHA256.</li>
<li class="">It signs the canonical request with that key, and adds the signature to the URL.</li>
</ol>
<p>For an upload, the URL also requires the request to send that exact <code>Content-Type</code> header, and a body of exactly the size that the module signed. The bucket rejects any other upload.</p>
<p>Set the constants at the top to your bucket&#x27;s. With AWS, the host includes the bucket&#x27;s name, and the bucket path is empty. Services that put the bucket in the path instead, such as Cloudflare R2, take their own host, and the bucket path <code>/your-bucket</code>.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Your bucket. With AWS: &#x27;<bucket>.s3.\<region\>.amazonaws.com&#x27;, and an empty path.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3_HOST </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my-bucket.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3_BUCKET_PATH </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3_REGION </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">us-east-1</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 300</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 5 minutes</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Returns a URL that allows `method` requests on `key`, until it expires.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// For an upload, pass the content type and the size: the request must send that</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// `Content-Type` header, and a body of exactly `contentLength` bytes.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">method</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">contentLength</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">  // For example, 20260115T093000Z.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> amzDate </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.timestamp.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">toDate</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">toISOString</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">replace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF5555">/</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">[</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">-:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">]</span><span style="color:#FF5555">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">g</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">).</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">replace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF5555">/</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">\.</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">\d</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+</span><span style="color:#FF5555">/</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> date </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> amzDate.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">slice</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">8</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> scope </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">date</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_REGION</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/s3/aws4_request`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> path </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_BUCKET_PATH</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> headers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> contentType</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    ?</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `content-length:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">contentLength</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-type:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">host:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_HOST</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    :</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `host:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_HOST</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signedHeaders </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> contentType </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">?</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-length;content-type;host</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> :</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">host</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> query </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">    &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">    `X-Amz-Credential=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">encodeURIComponent</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.env.</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">scope</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">    `X-Amz-Date=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">amzDate</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">    `X-Amz-Expires=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">    `X-Amz-SignedHeaders=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">encodeURIComponent</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(signedHeaders)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> canonicalRequest </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [method, path, query, headers, signedHeaders, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> stringToSign </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">    &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">AWS4-HMAC-SHA256</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    amzDate,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    scope,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    bytesToHex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">utf8ToBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(canonicalRequest))),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ].</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signingKey </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> utf8ToBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`AWS4</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.env.</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> part </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [date, S3_REGION, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">s3</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">aws4_request</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    signingKey </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> hmac</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(sha256, signingKey, </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">utf8ToBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(part));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signature </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> bytesToHex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">hmac</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(sha256, signingKey, </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">utf8ToBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(stringToSign)));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  return</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `https://</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_HOST</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">?</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&X-Amz-Signature=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">signature</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Your bucket. With AWS: "<bucket>.s3.\<region\>.amazonaws.com", and an empty path.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3Host </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my-bucket.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3BucketPath </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> S3Region </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">us-east-1</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> UrlExpirySeconds </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 300</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 5 minutes</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Returns a URL that allows `method` requests on `key`, until it expires.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // For an upload, pass the content type and the size: the request must send that</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // `Content-Type` header, and a body of exactly `contentLength` bytes.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> method</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> contentLength</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">        // For example, 20260115T093000Z.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> amzDate </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ((</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DateTimeOffset</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)ctx.Timestamp).UtcDateTime.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToString</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">yyyyMMdd&#x27;T&#x27;HHmmss&#x27;Z&#x27;</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> date </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> amzDate[</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">8</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> scope </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> $"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">date</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3Region</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/s3/aws4_request</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> path </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> $"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3BucketPath</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> headers </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> contentType </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            ?</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-length:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">contentLength</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-type:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">host:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3Host</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            :</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">host:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3Host</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signedHeaders </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> contentType </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ?</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-length;content-type;host</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> :</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">host</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> query </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">            "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">            $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">X-Amz-Credential=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Uri</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">EscapeDataString</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Env</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">scope</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">            $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">X-Amz-Date=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">amzDate</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">            $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">X-Amz-Expires=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">UrlExpirySeconds</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#E9F284">            $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">X-Amz-SignedHeaders=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Uri</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">EscapeDataString</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">(</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">signedHeaders</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> canonicalRequest </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, method, path, query, headers, signedHeaders, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> stringToSign </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">AWS4-HMAC-SHA256</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, amzDate, scope, </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Hex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(canonicalRequest)));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signingKey </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Encoding.UTF8.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">AWS4</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Env</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        foreach</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> part </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">in</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[] { date, S3Region, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">s3</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">aws4_request</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            signingKey </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> HmacSha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(signingKey, part);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signature </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Hex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">HmacSha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(signingKey, stringToSign));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> $"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">https://</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">S3Host</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">?</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">query</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&X-Amz-Signature=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">signature</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> bytes </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Encoding.UTF8.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(data);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> digest </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Sha256Digest</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        digest.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">BlockUpdate</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(bytes, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, bytes.Length);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> hash </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> byte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[digest.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetDigestSize</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        digest.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">DoFinal</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(hash, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> hash;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> byte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[] </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">HmacSha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">byte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[] </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> data</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> bytes </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Encoding.UTF8.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetBytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(data);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> mac </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> HMac</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Sha256Digest</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        mac.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Init</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> KeyParameter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        mac.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">BlockUpdate</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(bytes, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, bytes.Length);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> byte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[mac.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetMacSize</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        mac.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">DoFinal</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(result, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Hex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">byte</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[] </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">bytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Convert.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToHexString</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(bytes).</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToLowerInvariant</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
