With AWS: "<bucket>.s3.\<region\>.amazonaws.com", and an empty path.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> S3_HOST</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "my-bucket.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> S3_BUCKET_PATH</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> S3_REGION</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "us-east-1"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u32</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 300</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 5 minutes</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// Returns a URL that allows `method` requests on `key`, until it expires.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// For an upload, pass the content type and the size: the request must send that</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// `Content-Type` header, and a body of exactly `content_length` bytes.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, method</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, content_length</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // For example, 20260115T093000Z.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> now </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">timestamp</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_rfc3339</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">expect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"the time can be formatted"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> amz_date </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{}T{}Z"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, now[</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">10</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">replace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&#x27;-&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">), now[</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">11</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">19</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">replace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&#x27;:&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> date </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">amz_date[</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">8</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">];</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> scope </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{date}/{S3_REGION}/s3/aws4_request"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> path </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{S3_BUCKET_PATH}/{key}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (headers, signed_headers) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        (</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"host:{S3_HOST}</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">), </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"host"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        (</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">            format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"content-length:{content_length}</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-type:{content_type}</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">host:{S3_HOST}</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">            "content-length;content-type;host"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        )</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> credential </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{}/{scope}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">env</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">S3_ACCESS_KEY_ID</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> query </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">        "X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">        format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"X-Amz-Credential={}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, credential</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">replace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&#x27;/&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"%2F"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">        format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"X-Amz-Date={amz_date}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">        format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"X-Amz-Expires={URL_EXPIRY_SECONDS}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">        format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"X-Amz-SignedHeaders={}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, signed_headers</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">replace</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&#x27;;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"%3B"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"&"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> canonical_request </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [method, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">query, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">headers, signed_headers, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"UNSIGNED-PAYLOAD"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> string_to_sign </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F1FA8C">        "AWS4-HMAC-SHA256"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        &</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">amz_date,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        &</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">scope,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        &</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">hex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Sha256</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">digest</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(canonical_request</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">as_bytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">join</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">\n</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> mut</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signing_key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"AWS4{}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">env</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">S3_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">into_bytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> part </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">in</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> [date, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">S3_REGION</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"s3"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"aws4_request"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">] {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        signing_key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> hmac_sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">signing_key, part);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> signature </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> hex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">hmac_sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">signing_key, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">string_to_sign));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"https://{S3_HOST}{path}?{query}&X-Amz-Signature={signature}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> hmac_sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">u8</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">], data</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">u8</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> mut</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> mac </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Hmac</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Sha256</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">></span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new_from_slice</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">expect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"HMAC accepts keys of any length"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    mac</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">update</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(data</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">as_bytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    mac</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">finalize</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">into_bytes</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> hex</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(bytes</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">u8</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    bytes</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">map</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">byte</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{byte:02x}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">))</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>The file keys that your module chooses only contain letters, digits and slashes, so <code>presign</code> doesn&#x27;t need to URL-encode them.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="request-an-upload">Request an upload<a href="#request-an-upload" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Request an upload" title="Direct link to Request an upload" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add a <code>request_upload</code> procedure that clients call with the file&#x27;s name, its content type, such as <code>image/png</code>, and its size in bytes. It:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">Rejects names that are empty or longer than 200 characters, content types that aren&#x27;t a type and a subtype, such as <code>image/png</code>, and files bigger than 50 MB.</li>
<li class="">Chooses the file&#x27;s key from the caller&#x27;s identity and the current time, such as <code>uploads/c200…/1767864600000000</code>. Players can&#x27;t choose where their file goes, so they can&#x27;t overwrite anyone else&#x27;s.</li>
<li class="">Records the upload in <code>pending_upload</code>, and returns the key and a signed <code>PUT</code> URL. The URL only accepts a file of that size and content type, so a player can&#x27;t upload a bigger file than the one they asked for.</li>
</ul>
<p>The procedure returns an <code>UploadTicket</code> value with three fields: <code>key</code>, <code>url</code>, and <code>error</code>, which explains what went wrong, or is empty on success. Returning errors this way, instead of throwing, means clients get the same kind of result whichever language your module is written in.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_NAME_LENGTH </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 200</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_FILE_SIZE </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 50</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 50 MB</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> uploadTicket </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">object</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">UploadTicket</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> requestUpload </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  uploadTicket,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">contentType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">size</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (name.length </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name.length </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_NAME_LENGTH) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `File names must be 1 to </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">MAX_NAME_LENGTH</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> characters long`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#FF5555">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">^</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">[</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">\w.</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">+-</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">]</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">\/</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">[</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">\w.</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">+-</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">]</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+$</span><span style="color:#FF5555">/</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">test</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(contentType)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">That doesn&#x27;t look like a content type</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (size </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> BigInt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(MAX_FILE_SIZE)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `Files must be at most </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">MAX_FILE_SIZE</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> /</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1024</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> MB`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `uploads/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.sender.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">toHexString</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">/</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx.timestamp.microsSinceUnixEpoch</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      tx.db.pendingUpload.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ key, owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.sender, name, contentType, requestedAt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.timestamp });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { key, url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PUT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, key, contentType, size), error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MaxNameLength </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 200</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MaxFileSize </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 50</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 50 MB</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadTicket</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        url,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// True for a type and a subtype, such as "image/png".</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> is_content_type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> bool</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> is_token </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> |</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">part</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">str</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        !</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">part</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> part</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">chars</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">all</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">c</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> c</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_ascii_alphanumeric</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "_.+-"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">contains</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(c))</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">split_once</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">&#x27;/&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>

<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"></path></svg></span>Accepting only some types</div><div class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p>To accept only some kinds of files, such as images, check the content type against a list in <code>request_upload</code>. The content type is signed into the upload URL, so the bucket rejects an upload with a different <code>Content-Type</code> header. It&#x27;s still the type that the client says the file has: if you need to know what the file really contains, check its content after the upload.</p></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="confirm-the-upload">Confirm the upload<a href="#confirm-the-upload" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Confirm the upload" title="Direct link to Confirm the upload" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add an <code>s3_request</code> function (<code>s3Request</code> in TypeScript, <code>S3Request</code> in C#) that sends a signed request for a file to the bucket, and returns the status of the reply, or 0 if there was no reply. For a <code>HEAD</code> request, it also returns the file&#x27;s size.</p>
<p>Then add a <code>confirm_upload</code> procedure that clients call once their upload is done. It:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">Rejects keys that aren&#x27;t one of the caller&#x27;s pending uploads.</li>
<li class="">Sends a <code>HEAD</code> request for the file, which returns its size without downloading it. It rejects the upload if the file isn&#x27;t in the bucket.</li>
<li class="">As a second check, if the file is bigger than 50 MB, deletes it from the bucket and from <code>pending_upload</code>, and rejects the upload.</li>
<li class="">Otherwise, moves the upload from <code>pending_upload</code> to <code>uploaded_file</code>, in one transaction.</li>
</ul>
<p>It returns an error message, or an empty string on success.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Sends a signed request for `key` to the bucket.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Returns the status of the reply, or 0 if there was no reply, and the size of the file.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> s3Request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">method</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  try</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> response </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.http.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">fetch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, method, key), {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      method,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      timeout</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> TimeDuration.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">fromMillis</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">10_000</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { status</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> response.status, size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> Number</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(response.headers.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">get</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-length</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">??</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">catch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not reach the bucket: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { status</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> confirmUpload </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() }, t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.db.pendingUpload.key.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">pending </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> !</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">pending.owner.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">isEqual</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Upload not found</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> head </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> s3Request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">HEAD</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (head.status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!==</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 200</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> head.status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">===</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 404</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ?</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">The file was not uploaded</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> :</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not check the file</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (head.size </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> MAX_FILE_SIZE) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">    s3Request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">DELETE</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.db.pendingUpload.key.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> `Files must be at most </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">MAX_FILE_SIZE</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> /</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1024</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> MB`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Do nothing if another call already confirmed this upload.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (tx.db.pendingUpload.key.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      tx.db.uploadedFile.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending.owner,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        key,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending.name,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        contentType</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending.contentType,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> BigInt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(head.size),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        uploadedAt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Sends a signed request for `key` to the bucket.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Returns the status of the reply, or 0 if there was no reply, and the size of the file.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    static</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">int</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Status, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Size) </span><span style="color:#50FA7B">S3Request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">HttpMethod</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> method</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        };</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> size </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> length.Value </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> null</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ?</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> :</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Parse</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Encoding.UTF8.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetString</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(length.Value));</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">body</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">expect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"the request is valid"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">and_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">value</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> value</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">parse</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[procedure]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> confirm_upload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &mut</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">pending_upload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(pending) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">pending</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">owner </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "Upload not found"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (status, size) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> s3_request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"HEAD"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 200</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 404</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"The file was not uploaded"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not check the file"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> size </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">></span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> MAX_FILE_SIZE</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">        s3_request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"DELETE"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">pending_upload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> format!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Files must be at most {} MB"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">MAX_FILE_SIZE</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> /</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">1024</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1024</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">        // Do nothing if another call already confirmed this upload.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">pending_upload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">uploaded_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">UploadedFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                owner</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">owner,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">clone</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">clone</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> pending</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">clone</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                size,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                uploaded_at</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">timestamp,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">    String</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>Clients can only confirm their own uploads, and only with the size that the bucket reports, so the <code>uploaded_file</code> table only lists files that are really in the bucket.</p>
<p>Until the upload URL expires, 5 minutes after <code>request_upload</code>, the player can use it again. They can only send a file of the same size and type to the same key, so at most they replace their own file. If they delete the file in that time, they can also put it back in the bucket, without a row in <code>uploaded_file</code>.</p>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="download-and-delete-files">Download and delete files<a href="#download-and-delete-files" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Download and delete files" title="Direct link to Download and delete files" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>Add two more procedures:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><code>request_download</code> returns a signed <code>GET</code> URL for a file in <code>uploaded_file</code>. Any player can download any file, as with a public file list. To limit who can, check the caller first, as <code>delete_file</code> does.</li>
<li class=""><code>delete_file</code> deletes a file from the bucket, then from <code>uploaded_file</code>. Only the file&#x27;s owner can delete it. For other players, it answers as if the file didn&#x27;t exist.</li>
</ul>
<p><code>request_download</code> returns a <code>DownloadLink</code> value, with a <code>url</code> and an <code>error</code>. <code>delete_file</code> returns an error message, or an empty string on success.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> downloadLink </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">object</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">DownloadLink</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> requestDownload </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ fileId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() }, downloadLink, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">fileId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.db.uploadedFile.id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">File not found</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">GET</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, row.key), error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> deleteFile </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ fileId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() }, t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, { </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">fileId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> }) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.db.uploadedFile.id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> !</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row.owner.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">isEqual</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx.sender)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">File not found</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { status } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> s3Request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">DELETE</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, row.key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 200</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">>=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 300</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not delete the file</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.db.uploadedFile.id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span></code></pre></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> class</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD"> Module</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Type</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> partial</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> struct</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> DownloadLink</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> Url;</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> DownloadLink</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> RequestDownload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> fileId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WithTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.Db.UploadedFile.Id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId)) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> not</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadedFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> string</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> DeleteFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> fileId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WithTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.Db.UploadedFile.Id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId)) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> not</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadedFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row.Owner </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.Sender)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 200</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">>=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 300</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WithTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.Db.UploadedFile.Id.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>

<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[procedure]</span></span>
