<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> DownloadLink</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> presign</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"GET"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#BD93F9">0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        None</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> DownloadLink</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "File not found"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[procedure]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> delete_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &mut</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, file_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-></span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">uploaded_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">id</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">find</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(file_id));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Some</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(row) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">owner </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">==</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">sender</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">else</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "File not found"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (status, _) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> s3_request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"DELETE"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">row</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> !</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">200</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">300</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">contains</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">status) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> "Could not delete the file"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">uploaded_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">id</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(file_id));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">    String</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="clean-up-abandoned-uploads">Clean up abandoned uploads<a href="#clean-up-abandoned-uploads" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Clean up abandoned uploads" title="Direct link to Clean up abandoned uploads" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>A player can request an upload and never confirm it, for example because they closed the game halfway through. Its row stays in <code>pending_upload</code>, and its file, if they sent one, stays in the bucket.</p>
<p>Insert a row into <code>upload_cleanup</code> from the <code>init</code> reducer, so that SpacetimeDB calls <code>clean_up_uploads</code> every hour. Then add the <code>clean_up_uploads</code> procedure. It finds the pending uploads that were requested more than an hour ago, and for each of them:</p>
\<ol\>
<li class="">Deletes its row from <code>pending_upload</code>. From then on, <code>confirm_upload</code> rejects it, so it can&#x27;t list a file that&#x27;s about to be deleted.</li>
<li class="">Deletes its file from the bucket. Deleting a file that was never uploaded succeeds too, so there&#x27;s no need to check first.</li>
</ol>
<p>An upload URL expires after 5 minutes, so an hour leaves plenty of time for a slow upload to finish and be confirmed.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MICROS </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 60</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 60</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1_000_000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 1 hour</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ABANDONED_AFTER_MICROS </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 60</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 60</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 1_000_000</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">; </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 1 hour</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> init </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">init</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  ctx.db.uploadCleanup.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    scheduledId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">n</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    scheduledAt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ScheduleAt.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">interval</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MICROS),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">});</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">export</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> cleanUpUploads </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { onSchedule</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> uploadCleanup },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  { cleanup</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> uploadCleanup.rowType },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  t.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unit</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">  ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> cutoff </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.timestamp.microsSinceUnixEpoch </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ABANDONED_AFTER_MICROS;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> abandoned </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">withTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      Array.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(tx.db.pendingUpload.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> upload.requestedAt.microsSinceUnixEpoch </span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> cutoff)</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> abandoned) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            ScheduledAt </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ScheduleAt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Interval</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(CleanupInterval),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    [</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Procedure</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    public</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> static</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> void</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> CleanUpUploads</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">UploadCleanup</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> cleanup</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Where</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> upload.RequestedAt.MicrosecondsSinceUnixEpoch </span><span style="color:#FF79C6"><</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> cutoff)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Select</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> upload.Key)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ToList</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">());</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        foreach</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">in</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> abandoned)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WithTx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx.Db.PendingUpload.Key.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(key)))</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> ABANDONED_AFTER</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Duration</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Duration</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">from_secs</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">60</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> *</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 60</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">); </span><span style="color:#6272A4">// 1 hour</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[reducer(init)]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> init</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">ReducerContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">upload_cleanup</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">insert</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">UploadCleanup</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        scheduled_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> 0</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        scheduled_at</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ScheduleAt</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Interval</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#BD93F9">CLEANUP_INTERVAL</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">into</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">#[procedure]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">pub</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> clean_up_uploads</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &mut</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ProcedureContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, _cleanup</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadCleanup</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> cutoff </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">timestamp</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_micros_since_unix_epoch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">-</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> ABANDONED_AFTER</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">as_micros</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">as</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> i64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> abandoned</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Vec</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">pending_upload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">filter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">requested_at</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_micros_since_unix_epoch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() < cutoff)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">map</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">collect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> key </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">in</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> abandoned {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_tx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> tx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">db</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">pending_upload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">key</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key)) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">            s3_request</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ctx, </span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"DELETE"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<p>If the bucket can&#x27;t be reached, <code>s3_request</code> (<code>s3Request</code> in TypeScript, <code>S3Request</code> in C#) logs the error, and the file stays in the bucket without a row. It no longer appears anywhere in your database, so it&#x27;s harmless, but it still uses storage.</p>
<div class="theme-admonition theme-admonition-note admonition_Ph9R alert alert--secondary"><div class="admonitionHeading_kG9t"><span class="admonitionIcon_VA_f"><svg viewBox="0 0 14 16"><path fill-rule="evenodd" d="M6.3 5.69a.942.942 0 0 1-.28-.7c0-.28.09-.52.28-.7.19-.18.42-.28.7-.28.28 0 .52.09.7.28.18.19.28.42.28.7 0 .28-.09.52-.28.7a1 1 0 0 1-.7.3c-.28 0-.52-.11-.7-.3zM8 7.99c-.02-.25-.11-.48-.31-.69-.2-.19-.42-.3-.69-.31H6c-.27.02-.48.13-.69.31-.2.2-.3.44-.31.69h1v3c.02.27.11.5.31.69.2.2.42.31.69.31h1c.27 0 .48-.11.69-.31.2-.19.3-.42.31-.69H8V7.98v.01zM7 2.3c-3.14 0-5.7 2.54-5.7 5.68 0 3.14 2.56 5.7 5.7 5.7s5.7-2.55 5.7-5.7c0-3.15-2.56-5.69-5.7-5.69v.01zM7 .98c3.86 0 7 3.14 7 7s-3.14 7-7 7-7-3.12-7-7 3.14-7 7-7z"></path></svg></span>note</div><div class="admonitionContent_STxx"><p><code>init</code> only runs when the database is created. Publishing new code to an existing database doesn&#x27;t run it again, so the cleanup won&#x27;t start there. To start it, either republish with <code>--delete-data</code>, which erases all data, or insert the row from another reducer. Before inserting, check that <code>upload_cleanup</code> is empty: with two rows, <code>clean_up_uploads</code> would run twice as often.</p></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="upload-from-the-client">Upload from the client<a href="#upload-from-the-client" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to Upload from the client" title="Direct link to Upload from the client" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>On the client, subscribe to <code>uploaded_file</code> to list the files. To upload a file:</p>
\<ol\>
<li class="">Call <code>request_upload</code> with the file&#x27;s name, content type and size.</li>
<li class="">Send the file to the returned URL with an HTTP <code>PUT</code>, and a <code>Content-Type</code> header with the same content type. HTTP libraries send the file&#x27;s size in a <code>Content-Length</code> header themselves, and it must match the size you passed.</li>
<li class="">Call <code>confirm_upload</code> with the returned key.</li>
</ol>
<p>To download a file, call <code>request_download</code>, and open or fetch the returned URL within 5 minutes.</p>
<div class="theme-tabs-container tabs-container tabList_duiv"><ul role="tablist" aria-orientation="horizontal" class="tabs"><li role="tab" tabindex="0" aria-selected="true" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9 tabs__item--active">TypeScript</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">C#</li><li role="tab" tabindex="-1" aria-selected="false" class="tabs__item tabItem_a1H9">Rust</li></ul><div class="margin-top--md"><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI"><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">import</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { DbConnection, tables } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">from</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">./module_bindings</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    ctx.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">subscriptionBuilder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">().</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">subscribe</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">([tables.uploadedFile]);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">build</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> file </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">of</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.db.uploadedFile.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">log</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">file.id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">file.name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">file.size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> bytes)`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Uploads a file, such as one the player picked in a file input.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">async</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> uploadFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">file</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> File</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> contentType </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> file.type </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">application/octet-stream</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  try</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ticket </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> await</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">requestUpload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> file.name, contentType, size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> BigInt</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(file.size) });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (ticket.error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not upload the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ticket.error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> response </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> await</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> fetch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket.url, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      method</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">PUT</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      headers</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> { </span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">content-type</span><span style="color:#E9F284">&#x27;</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> contentType },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      body</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> file,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">response.ok) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`The upload failed with status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">response.status</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> error </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> await</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">confirmUpload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ key</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ticket.key });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not upload the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">catch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error) {</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Returns a URL to download the file from, valid for 5 minutes.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">async</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> downloadUrl</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">fileId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> bigint</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  try</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> link </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> await</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">requestDownload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ fileId });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (link.error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not download the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">link.error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">      return</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> undefined</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> link.url;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">catch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not call the procedure: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    return</span><span style="color:#BD93F9"> undefined</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">async</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> function</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> deleteFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">fileId</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> bigint</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">  try</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    const</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> error </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> await</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">deleteFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">({ fileId });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">      console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not delete the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  } </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">catch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">error</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`Could not call the procedure: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">${</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">`</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">  }</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> System</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Net</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Http</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Headers</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> System</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Threading</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Tasks</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">using</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> SpacetimeDB</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">.</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Types</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> http </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> HttpClient</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> DbConnection.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Builder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WithUri</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">http://localhost:3000</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WithDatabaseName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">my-game</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">OnConnect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">((</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">conn</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">identity</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">token</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        conn.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">SubscriptionBuilder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">AddQuery</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">q</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> q.From.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">UploadedFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Subscribe</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Build</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
