<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    foreach</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> file </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">in</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn.Db.UploadedFile.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">Iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">file</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">file</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Name</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">file</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Size</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C"> bytes)</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Uploads the file at `path`.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">void</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> UploadFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> path</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> contentType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> size </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> FileInfo</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(path).Length;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn.Procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">RequestUpload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(Path.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">GetFileName</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(path), contentType, size, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result.IsSuccess </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result.Value </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> not</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> UploadTicket</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ticket)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not call the procedure: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        else</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (ticket.Error </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not upload the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ticket</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        else</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            _ </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> PutAndConfirm</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket, path, contentType);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">async</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Task</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> PutAndConfirm</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">UploadTicket</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> ticket</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> path</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> contentType</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">    HttpResponseMessage</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> response;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    try</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        var</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> content </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> ByteArrayContent</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">await</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> File.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ReadAllBytesAsync</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(path));</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        content.Headers.ContentType </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> new</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> MediaTypeHeaderValue</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(contentType);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        response </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> await</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> http.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">PutAsync</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket.Url, content);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    catch</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Exception</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">when</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (error </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> HttpRequestException</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> or</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> IOException</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> or</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> TaskCanceledException</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">        // The file couldn&#x27;t be read, or the bucket couldn&#x27;t be reached.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">The upload failed: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Message</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">response.IsSuccessStatusCode)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">The upload failed with status </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">int</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">)</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">response</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">StatusCode</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        return</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn.Procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">ConfirmUpload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket.Key, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result.IsSuccess)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not call the procedure: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        else</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (result.Value </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not upload the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Value</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">// Calls `onUrl` with a URL to download the file from, valid for 5 minutes.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">void</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> RequestDownload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> fileId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Action</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">string</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">> </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">onUrl</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn.Procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">RequestDownload</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result.IsSuccess </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result.Value </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">is</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> not</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> DownloadLink</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> link)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not call the procedure: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        else</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (link.Error </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not download the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">link</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        else</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">            onUrl</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(link.Url);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">void</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> DeleteFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">ulong</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic"> fileId</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn.Procedures.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">DeleteFile</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(fileId, (</span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">ctx</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FFB86C;font-style:italic">result</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result.IsSuccess)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not call the procedure: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        else</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> (result.Value </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">!=</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> ""</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            Console.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">WriteLine</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#E9F284">$"</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">Could not delete the file: </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">{</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">result</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">Value</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">}</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Remember to call <code>conn.FrameTick()</code> regularly, for example once per frame, so the callbacks run. In Unity, send the file with <code>UnityWebRequest.Put</code> instead of <code>HttpClient</code>.</p></div><div role="tabpanel" class="tabItem_w6aI" hidden=""><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">mod</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> module_bindings;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> module_bindings</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::*</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> spacetimedb_sdk</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">{</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbContext</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Table</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">};</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Path</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">use</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">sync</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Arc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">;</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> main</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Arc</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        DbConnection</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">builder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_uri</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"http://localhost:3000"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">with_database_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"my-game"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">on_connect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ctx, _identity, _token</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">                ctx</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">subscription_builder</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">add_query</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">q</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> q</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">from</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">uploaded_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">())</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">subscribe</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            })</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">build</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            .</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">expect</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"failed to connect"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    );</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Process messages from the database on a background thread.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">run_threaded</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">    // Your game loop runs here.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> list_files</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    for</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> file </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">in</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">db</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">uploaded_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">iter</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#50FA7B">        println!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"{}: {} ({} bytes)"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, file</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">id, file</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">name, file</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">size);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// Uploads the file at `path`.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> upload_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">Arc</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"><</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">>, path</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> name </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Path</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">new</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">file_name</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">unwrap_or_default</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">to_string_lossy</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">()</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">into_owned</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> size </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">fs</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">metadata</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(metadata) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> metadata</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">len</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not read the file: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">    let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn_for_upload </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">clone</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">();</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">procedures</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">request_upload_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(name, content_type</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">clone</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(), size, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">move</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> |</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">        let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ticket </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">            Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ticket</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ticket,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">            Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not upload the file: {}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, ticket</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">            Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not call the procedure: {error:?}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">        // Send the file on another thread, so the callback doesn&#x27;t hold up the connection.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">thread</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">spawn</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">move</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> ||</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            let</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> body </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> std</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">fs</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">read</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">path) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">                Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(body) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> body,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">                Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not read the file: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            };</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">            if</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> let</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> ureq</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">::</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">put</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">ticket</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">url)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">header</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"content-type"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">content_type)</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">send</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">&</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">body[</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">..</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">]) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">                return</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"The upload failed: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            }</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            conn_for_upload</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">procedures</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">confirm_upload_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(ticket</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">key, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">                Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">                Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not upload the file: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">                Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not call the procedure: {error:?}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">            });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">        });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#6272A4">/// Calls `on_url` with a URL to download the file from, valid for 5 minutes.</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> request_download</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, file_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, on_url</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> impl</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> FnOnce</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">String</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">+</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> Send</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> +</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> &#x27;</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">static</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">procedures</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">request_download_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(file_id, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">move</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> |</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(link) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> link</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> on_url</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(link</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">url),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(link) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not download the file: {}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, link</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">error),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not call the procedure: {error:?}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">fn</span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> delete_file</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> &</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">DbConnection</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">, file_id</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">:</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic"> u64</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    conn</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">procedures</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">delete_file_then</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(file_id, </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">_ctx, result</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">|</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> match</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> result {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">if</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> error</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">.</span><span style="color:#50FA7B">is_empty</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">() </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#F8F8F2"> {}</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Ok</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not delete the file: {error}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD;font-style:italic">        Err</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(error) </span><span style="color:#FF79C6">=></span><span style="color:#50FA7B"> eprintln!</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">(</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">"Could not call the procedure: {error:?}"</span><span style="color:#F8F8F2">),</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">    });</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#F8F8F2">}</span></span></code></pre><p>Add <code>ureq</code> to your client&#x27;s <code>Cargo.toml</code>, to send the file:</p><pre tabindex="0" class="codeBlockStandalone_LlrK thin-scrollbar codeBlockContainer_hFbQ theme-code-block" style="--prism-color:#bfc7d5;--prism-background-color:#292d3e"><code class="codeBlockLines_JqGU"><span class="line"><span style="color:#FF79C6">[</span><span style="color:#8BE9FD">dependencies</span><span style="color:#FF79C6">]</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#8BE9FD">ureq</span><span style="color:#FF79C6"> =</span><span style="color:#E9F284"> "</span><span style="color:#F1FA8C">3</span><span style="color:#E9F284">"</span></span></code></pre></div></div></div>
<h2 class="anchor anchorTargetStickyNavbar_Y_NL" id="whats-next">What&#x27;s next?<a href="#whats-next" class="hash-link" aria-label="Direct link to What&#x27;s next?" title="Direct link to What&#x27;s next?" translate="no">​</a></h2>
<p>You now store players&#x27; files in a bucket, and keep track of them in your database:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class="">Clients upload and download files directly from the bucket, with URLs that your module signs and that expire after 5 minutes.</li>
<li class="">Your module chooses where each file goes and how big it can be, and checks that it&#x27;s in the bucket before it lists it.</li>
<li class="">A scheduled procedure deletes the uploads that players never confirmed.</li>
<li class="">The bucket stays private, and your S3 keys never leave the database.</li>
</ul>
<p>To adapt it to your app:</p>
\<ul\>
<li class=""><strong>Limit who can download.</strong> Check the caller in <code>request_download</code>, for example to let only the owner download a file, or the owner&#x27;s friends, as in <a class="" href="/docs/how-to/social/friends-list">Build a friends or follow list</a>. To also hide the file list, make <code>uploaded_file</code> private and give each player their own files through a <a class="" href="/docs/functions/views">view</a>.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Other S3-compatible services.</strong> With Cloudflare R2, set the host to <code><account-id>.r2.cloudflarestorage.com</code>, the bucket path to <code>/your-bucket</code>, and the region to <code>auto</code>.</li>
<li class=""><strong>Public files.</strong> To serve files that anyone can see, such as avatars, make their part of the bucket public, or put a CDN in front of it, and store the public URL in <code>uploaded_file</code> instead of signing download URLs.</li>
</ul>
<p>To learn more about the features used here, see <a class="" href="/docs/functions/procedures">Procedures</a>, <a class="" href="/docs/databases/environment-variables">Environment Variables</a> and <a class="" href="/docs/tables/file-storage">File Storage</a>.</p>
<p>Next, you can <a class="" href="/docs/how-to/social/unique-usernames">keep usernames unique and block offensive ones</a>.</p></div><footer class="theme-doc-footer docusaurus-mt-lg"><div class="row
